Viṣṇu Purāṇa

uttaraṃ yad agastyasya ajavīthyāś ca dakṣiṇam | pitṛyānaḥ sa vai panthā vaiśvānarapathād bahiḥ ||85||

Śrīdhara

tatra pitṛyānam āha—uttaram iti sārddhaiḥ pañcabhiḥ | tatra jyotiścakre vīthīkramo vāyunoktaḥ— sarvagrahāṇāṃ trīṇyeva sthānāni dvijasattama | sthānaṃ jārodravaṃ madhyaṃ tathairāvatamuttaram | vaiśvānaraṃ dakṣiṇato nirdiṣṭamiha tattvataḥ || tad evamadhyamottaradakṣiṇamārgatrayaṃ pratyekaṃ vīthītrayeṇa tridhā bhidyate | tathāhi | tribhistribhiraścinyādinakṣatrairnāgavīthī gajavīthī airāvatī cety uttaramārge vīthītrayam | ārṣabhāge vīthī jārodgavī ceti vaiṣuvatamadhyamamārge vīthītrayam | ajavīthī mṛgavīthī vaiśvānarī ceti | dakṣiṇamārge vīthītrayam | taduktaṃ tatraiva— aśvinī kṛttikā yāmyā nāgavīthīti śabditā | rohiṇyārdrā mṛgaśiro gajavīthyabhidhīyate | puṣyāśleṣā tathādityā vīthī cairāvatī smṛtā | etāstu vīthayastisra uttaro mārga ucyate | tathā dve cāpi phalgunyau maghā caivārṣabhīmatā | hastā citrā tathā svātī govīthīti tu śabditā | jyeṣṭhā viśākhānurādhā vīthī jāradgavī matā | etāstu vīthayastisro madhyamo mārga ucyate | mūlāṣāḍhottarāṣāḍhā ajavīthyabhiśabditā | śravaṇā ca dhaniṣṭhā ca mārgo śatabhiṣak tathā | vaiśānarī bhādrapade revatī caiva kīrtitā | etāstu vīthayastisro dakṣiṇo mārga ucyate | iti || yāmyā bharaṇī ādityā aditidevatākā punarvasuḥ | mārgī mṛgavīthī evaṃ sthite āgastyāduttaramajavīthyāśca dakṣiṇam | agastyasya nikaṭavartino vaiśvānarapathād bahiḥ | vaiśvānaravīthīṃ varjayitvā mṛgavīthīmātraṃ pitṛyānamityarthaḥ ||85||

Devanagari
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

उत्तरं यद् अगस्त्यस्य अजवीथ्याश् च दक्षिणम् । पितृयानः स वै पन्था वैश्वानरपथाद् बहिः ॥८५॥

Śrīdhara

तत्र पितृयानम् आह—उत्तरम् इति सार्द्धैः पञ्चभिः । तत्र ज्योतिश्चक्रे वीथीक्रमो वायुनोक्तः— सर्वग्रहाणां त्रीण्येव स्थानानि द्विजसत्तम । स्थानं जारोद्रवं मध्यं तथैरावतमुत्तरम् । वैश्वानरं दक्षिणतो निर्दिष्टमिह तत्त्वतः ॥ तद् एवमध्यमोत्तरदक्षिणमार्गत्रयं प्रत्येकं वीथीत्रयेण त्रिधा भिद्यते । तथाहि । त्रिभिस्त्रिभिरश्चिन्यादिनक्षत्रैर्नागवीथी गजवीथी ऐरावती चेत्य् उत्तरमार्गे वीथीत्रयम् । आर्षभागे वीथी जारोद्गवी चेति वैषुवतमध्यममार्गे वीथीत्रयम् । अजवीथी मृगवीथी वैश्वानरी चेति । दक्षिणमार्गे वीथीत्रयम् । तदुक्तं तत्रैव— अश्विनी कृत्तिका याम्या नागवीथीति शब्दिता । रोहिण्यार्द्रा मृगशिरो गजवीथ्यभिधीयते । पुष्याश्लेषा तथादित्या वीथी चैरावती स्मृता । एतास्तु वीथयस्तिस्र उत्तरो मार्ग उच्यते । तथा द्वे चापि फल्गुन्यौ मघा चैवार्षभीमता । हस्ता चित्रा तथा स्वाती गोवीथीति तु शब्दिता । ज्येष्ठा विशाखानुराधा वीथी जारद्गवी मता । एतास्तु वीथयस्तिस्रो मध्यमो मार्ग उच्यते । मूलाषाढोत्तराषाढा अजवीथ्यभिशब्दिता । श्रवणा च धनिष्ठा च मार्गो शतभिषक् तथा । वैशानरी भाद्रपदे रेवती चैव कीर्तिता । एतास्तु वीथयस्तिस्रो दक्षिणो मार्ग उच्यते । इति ॥ याम्या भरणी आदित्या अदितिदेवताका पुनर्वसुः । मार्गी मृगवीथी एवं स्थिते आगस्त्यादुत्तरमजवीथ्याश्च दक्षिणम् । अगस्त्यस्य निकटवर्तिनो वैश्वानरपथाद् बहिः । वैश्वानरवीथीं वर्जयित्वा मृगवीथीमात्रं पितृयानमित्यर्थः ॥८५॥

VP 2.8.86

तत्रासते महात्मान ऋषयो येऽग्निहोत्रिणः । भूतारम्भकृतं ब्रह्म शंसन्तो ऋत्विग्उद्यताः । प्रारम्भन्ते तु ये लोकास् तेषां पन्थाः स दक्षिणः ॥८६॥

is termed a Yoga, or cycle. The years are severally called Samvatsara, Parivatsara, Idvatsara, Anuvatsara, and Vatsara. This is the time called a Yuga . The mountain range that

Śrīdhara translation

Those priests, ready to perform the rites, begin the actions when sacrifices have lapsed in another age. They praise the portion of the Veda that prescribes works of activity, declaring that Brahman’s work is the creation of beings and offspring.

चलितं विच्छिन्नसम्प्रदायं ब्रह्मवेदं स्थापयन्ति ॥८७॥

VP 2.8.88

जायमानास् तु पूर्वे च पश्चिमानां गृहेषु वै । पश्चिमाश् चैव पूर्वेषां जायन्ते निधनेष्व् इह ॥८८॥

The fathers are reborn in their children's families, and children are again reborn in their forefather's kula. The progeny continues like this.

Śrīdhara translation

Through lineage, prescribed boundaries, and the arrangements of varṇa and āśrama, the earlier fathers are born in the homes of later sons, and later sons in the homes of the earlier fathers when those fathers die, each according to their own eligibility. The śruti says: “A father may be born as his son, and a father may be born in a different womb.” Vāyu says: “When the Tretā Yuga arrives, the seven seers again set the varṇas and āśramas in their separate courses. The wise are born again and again in those same lineages; the father is born in the son, and the son in the father. Thus they continually revolve again and again through the Dvāpara ages, those who are knowers of the teachings of the kalpas and authors of the śāstras of knowledge.”

Devanagari
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

जायमानास् तु पूर्वे च पश्चिमानां गृहेषु वै । पश्चिमाश् चैव पूर्वेषां जायन्ते निधनेष्व् इह ॥८८॥

Śrīdhara

सन्तत्या वंशप्रवर्त्तनेन मर्यादाभिः वर्णाश्रमादिव्यवस्थाभिः, श्रुनेन शास्त्रप्रवर्त्तनेन च पूर्वे पितरः पश्चिमानां पुत्राणां, पश्चिमाः पुत्राश्च पूर्वेषां स्वपुत्रत्वेनोत्पन्नानां पितॄणां निधनेषु गृहेषु केनचिदंशेन स्वाधिकारवशात् जायन्ते । पिता पुत्रेण पितृमान्यो वियोनौऽ इति श्रुतेः । तथा च वायुः । प्राप्ते त्रेतायुगे चैव पुनः सप्तशयस्त्यिह । प्रवर्त्तयन्ति नान् वर्णानाश्रमांश्च पृथक्पृथक् । तेषामेवान्नये धीरा उत्पद्यन्ते पुनः पुनः । जायमानः पिता पुत्रे पुत्रः पितरि चैव हि ॥ एवमावर्त्तमानास्ते द्वापरेषु पुनः पुनः । कल्पानां भाष्यविद्यानां ज्ञानशास्त्रकृतश्च ये इति ॥८८॥

VP 2.8.90

first degree of Aries and of Libra, and making day and night of equal duration, or fifteen Muhúrttas each. When the sun, most excellent sage,

Śrīdhara translation

He describes the Devayāna in six verses beginning “Nāgavīthī.” The path of the gods lies north of the northern track called Nāgavīthī and south of the Seven Sages. Those who are self-controlled and have conquered their senses [follow it].

VP 2.8.92

Śrīdhara translation

The northern path, situated north of the Sun, is the route of Āryaman and Savitṛ; those who follow it reach that northern way. The form is Vedic metre.

अष्टाशीतिसहस्राणि मुनीनाम् ऊर्ध्वरेतसाम् । उदक्पन्थानम् अर्यम्णः स्थितान्य् आभूतसम्प्लवम् ॥९२॥

Śrīdhara

चार्य्यम्णः सवितुरुदक् पन्थानम् उत्तरं मार्गं स्थिताः श्रितानि । आकारश्छान्दसः ॥९२॥

VP 2.8.93

ऊर्ध्वोत्तरम् ऋषिभ्यस् तु ध्रुवो यत्र व्यवस्थितः । एतद् विष्णुपदं दिव्यं तृतीयं व्योम्नि भास्वरम् ॥९३॥

Because they have given up stree sanga, lobha, they don't have icchaa, dvesha, and the dualities, they are not interested in procreation.

Śrīdhara translation

It has been said that the path of the gods lies south of the Seven Sages. He now states Viṣṇu’s station above and beyond that: “above,” and so on, through the end. “Above” means higher than it and farther north. Where Dhruva stands north and above the Seven Sages is the divine third station, called Viṣṇupada, which supports Dhruva and is above the earth. That is the meaning. The Śrīdhara text repeats this explanation.

क्षीणा अशेषार्तेर् नानादेहप्राप्तेर् हेतवो येषां ते ॥९५॥

VP 2.8.96

धर्मं ध्रुवाद्यास् तिष्ठन्ति यत्र ते लोकसाक्षिणः । तत्सार्ष्ट्योत्पन्नयोगर्द्धास् तद्विष्णोः परमं पदम् ॥९६॥

Those who do Brahma hatyaa, a mahaa paapa; and those who perform Ashvamedha Yaaga, which is a punya; the fruits, paapa and punya respectively, are said to be present till the end of this kalpa. For one who does brahma hatyaa, his paapa will not get destroyed for such a long time. Those who do Ashvamedha yaaga, their punya makes them live for a long time.

Śrīdhara translation

By that similarity to Viṣṇu, through the power of yoga arising from similar discipline and mastery of the senses and the rest, they are illumined. The yoga prescribed by the teacher is regarded as yoga through practice; the statement of the Harivaṃśa shows that it would otherwise be difficult to explain.

VP 2.8.97

यत्रैतद् ओतं प्रोतं च यद् भूतं सचराचरम् । भाव्यं च विश्वं मैत्रेय तद् विष्णोः परमं पदम् ॥९७॥

All the worlds which are present till the Dhruva's place, only those places are destroyed - these three worlds. Aabhoota sambhava is naimittika pralaya; the three worlds will get destroyed during naimittika pralaya.

VP 2.8.98

दिवीव चक्षुर् आततं विततं तन् महात्मनाम् । विवेकज्ञानवृद्धं च तद् विष्णोः परमं पदम् ॥९८॥

Beyond these three worlds, and where Dhruva is also present, this is said to be Vishnu pada, which is very bright, shining, full of light. This is said to be the third place. Above that, where Dhruva is there, rishis live there. This is Vishnu Pada, the third world with respect to Pruthivi.

Śrīdhara translation

Spread through heaven, like the Sun-form eye, it is extended among the great ones as the illuminator of all. It is knowledge increased through discriminative knowledge—the understanding that distinguishes it from the phenomenal world—and is unlimited; that is the meaning. This indicates the meaning of the mantra “That is Viṣṇu’s.”

VP 2.8.99

gifts are meritorious. The sun is in his northern declination in the months Tapas, Tapasya, Madhu, Mádhava, Śukra, and Śuchi; and

Śrīdhara translation

By “where this is woven and stretched,” the universe is said to have its cause and support there. He now states how, by serving as Dhruva’s support, it nourishes the universe, in four verses beginning “in which.” “Bhāsvān” means luminous; “clouds” means water-bearing clouds.

VP 2.8.102

यत्रोत्तमेतत् प्रोतं च यद् भूतं सचराचरम् । भाव्यं च विश्वं मैत्रेय तद् विष्णोः परमं पदम् ॥१०२॥

What was there existing before, and what is going to come later on, created later; the three worlds, everything is controlled above the three worlds, are controlled like warp and weft of a cloth - the horizontal and vertical threads of a cloth. This is said to be Vishnu's Paramapada here.

VP 2.8.103

दिवीव चक्षुर् आततं योगिनां तन्मयात्मनाम् । विवेकज्ञानदृष्टं च तद् विष्णोः परमं पदम् ॥१०३॥

This shloka is similar to the Shruti mantra - Diveeva chakshuraatatam ... sadaa pashyanti soorayah, which is told with respect to the Nityasooris, who are there in saakshaat Sri Vaikuntha itself. No Śrīdhara Sanskrit passage is supplied here.

VP 2.8.105

मेघेषु सङ्गता वृष्टिर् वृष्टेः सृष्टेश् च पोषणम् । आप्यायनं च सर्वेषां देवादीनां महामुने ॥१०५॥

Now, Paraasharar tells about River Gangaa. From there, the Gangaa river flows, which destroys all the sins. It has got a beautiful brownish hue, because of paste present in the bodies of the Apsaras and maidens there.

Śrīdhara translation

By causing rain, it nourishes and increases creation through plants; this is satisfaction.

VP 2.8.106

ततश् चाज्याहुतिद्वारा पोषितास् ते हविर्भुजः । वृष्टेः कारणतां यान्ति भूतानां स्थितये पुनः ॥१०६॥

of ministrant priests: for as the worlds are destroyed and renewed, they institute new rules of conduct, and reestablish the

Śrīdhara translation

They are the cause of rain. This is stated in the Bhagavad Gītā: “Nourish the gods with this, and may the gods nourish you; nourishing one another, you will attain the highest good.”

Devanagari
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ततश् चाज्याहुतिद्वारा पोषितास् ते हविर्भुजः । वृष्टेः कारणतां यान्ति भूतानां स्थितये पुनः ॥१०६॥

Śrīdhara

वृष्टैः कारणतां यान्तीति । एतदुक्तं गीतासु । देवान् भावयतानेन ते देवा भावयन्तु वः । परस्परं भावयन्तः श्रेयः परमवाप्स्यथ ॥ इति ॥१०६॥

VP 2.8.107

एवम् एतत् पदं विष्णोस् तृतीयम् अमलात्मकम् । आधारभूतं लोकानां त्रयाणां वृष्टिकारणम् ॥१०७॥

interrupted ritual of the Vedas. Mutually descending from each other, progenitor springing from descendant, and descendant from progenitor, in

Śrīdhara translation

To show the world-purifying nature of Viṣṇupada, he says “thereafter,” and so on. From the station of Viṣṇupada the Ganga emerges in great splendour. Although she arose earlier from the opening in the cosmic egg pierced by Viṣṇu’s three-striding foot, from that point she becomes manifest as one served by the gods. He explains this: the waters and saffron ointments washed from the bodies of the goddesses during their water-play make her golden.

VP 2.8.108

ततः प्रभवति ब्रह्मन् सर्वपापहरा सरित् । गङ्गा देवाङ्गनाङ्गानाम् अनुलेपनपिञ्जरा ॥१०८॥

the alternating succession of births, they repeatedly appear in different housed and races along with their posterity, devout

Śrīdhara translation

He explains this: the waters and saffron ointments washed from the bodies of the goddesses during their water-play make her golden.

practices and instituted observances, residing to the south of the solar orb, as long as the moon and

Śrīdhara translation

She issued in the form of a stream from the thumbnail of Viṣṇu’s lotus-like left foot.

Śrīdhara translation

VP 2.8.111

the Nágavithi , and south of the seven Rishis. There dwell the Siddhas, of subdued senses, continent and pure,

Śrīdhara translation

The Seven Sages stand in her waters performing the Aghamarṣaṇa rite; their matted locks are carried and moved here and there by the current.

Using the waters of Gangaa, if they do mahaa yajnyaas, and worship Purushottama, the One who is aaraadhya by these yajnyaas, Yajnyesha. brahmins obtain great benefits and purushaarthaas, in this as well as other worlds. VP 2.8.120

श्रुताभिलषिता दृष्टा स्पृष्टा पीतावगाहिता । या पावयति भूतानि कीर्तिता च दिने दिने ॥१२०॥

Whether one hears about the greatness of the river Gangaa, or one gets a desire to take bath in Gangaa river, or one who sees the Gangaa itself, or one who touches the waters of Gangaa, one who drinks the waters of sacred Gangaa, one who does avagaaha snaana in it, one who even praises the great qualities and powers of the river Gangaa, all of them get purified, and sins get destroyed.

Śrīdhara translation

No Śrīdhara text for this verse in the source.

पीतावगाहिता (pītāvagāhitā) — drunk
या (yā) — bathed in
पावयति (pāvayati) — which
भूतानि (bhūtāni) — purifies
कीर्तिता (kīrtitā) — beings
च (ca) — praised
दिने (dine) — and
दिने (dine) — day after day

VP 2.8.121

गङ्गा गङ्गेति यैर् नाम योजनानां शतेष्व् अपि । स्थितैर् उच्चारितं हन्ति पापं जन्मत्रयार्जितम् ॥१२१॥

Those who are even hundred yojanaas away from Gangaa river, if they tell thrice as " Gangaa Gangaa Gangaa", they will be freed from sins accumulated from three janmaas.

Śrīdhara translation

No Śrīdhara text for this verse in the source.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa · word-for-word

गङ्गेति (gaṅgeti) — ‘Gaṅgā’
यैर् (yair) — by whom
नाम (nāma) — the name
योजनानां (yojanānāṃ) — of yojanas
शतेष्व् (śateṣv) — among hundreds
अपि (api) — even
स्थितैर् (sthitair) — while living far away
उच्चारितं (uccāritaṃ) — uttered
हन्ति (hanti) — destroys
पापं (pāpaṃ) — sin
जन्म-त्रयार्जितम् (janma-trayārjitam) — acquired over three births

VP 2.8.122

यतः सा पावनायालं त्रयाणां जगताम् अपि । समुद्भूता परं तत् तु तृतीयं भगवत्पदम् ॥१२२॥

are extinct, in consequence of the cessation of the consequences of piety or iniquity, and where they never sorrow more.

Śrīdhara translation

He concludes the discussion of Viṣṇupada: since the Ganga arose and became manifest from that place, it is the Bhagavān’s foot.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa · word-for-word
यतः (yataḥ) — because
सा (sā) — she
पावनायालं (pāvanāyālaṃ) — for purification
त्रयाणां (trayāṇāṃ) — is sufficient
जगताम् (jagatām) — of the three
अपि (api) — worlds
समुद्भूता (samudbhūtā) — even
परं (paraṃ) — having arisen
तत् (tat) — supreme
तु (tu) — that
तृतीयं (tṛtīyaṃ) — indeed
भगवत्-पदम् (bhagavat-padam) — the third abode of the Lord
प्रस्तुतं विष्णुपदं (prastutaṃ viṣṇupadaṃ) — the Viṣṇu-foot just discussed
निगमयति (nigamayati) — he concludes
यत इति (yata iti) — “from which,” thus
यतः स्थानात् (yataḥ sthānāt) — from which place
समुद्भता प्राकट्यं प्राप्ता (samudbhatā prākaṭyaṃ prāptā) — arisen and attained manifestation
तत् भगवत्पदम् इति (tat bhagavatpadam iti) — that is the Lord’s foot
Śrīdhara · word-for-word
प्रस्तुतं — the account
विष्णुपदं — Viṣṇupada
निगमयति — he concludes
यत — because
इति — thus

स्थानात् — from abode
प्राप्ता — obtained

इति — thus
Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

yataḥ sā pāvanāyālaṃ trayāṇāṃ jagatām api | samudbhūtā paraṃ tat tu tṛtīyaṃ bhagavatpadam ||122||

Śrīdhara

prastutaṃ viṣṇupadaṃ nigamayati—yata iti | yataḥ sthānāt samudbhatā prākaṭyaṃ prāptā, tat bhagavatpadam iti ||117|| iti śrīdharasvāmikṛtāyāṃ svaprakāśākhyāyāmātmaprakāśākhyāyāṃ vā śrīviṣṇupurāṇaṭīkāyāṃ dvitīyāṃśe aṣṭamo’dhyāyaḥ ||

Devanagari
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

यतः सा पावनायालं त्रयाणां जगताम् अपि । समुद्भूता परं तत् तु तृतीयं भगवत्पदम् ॥१२२॥

Śrīdhara

प्रस्तुतं विष्णुपदं निगमयति—यत इति । यतः स्थानात् समुद्भता प्राकट्यं प्राप्ता, तत् भगवत्पदम् इति ॥११७॥ इति श्रीधरस्वामिकृतायां स्वप्रकाशाख्यायामात्मप्रकाशाख्यायां वा श्रीविष्णुपुराणटीकायां द्वितीयांशे अष्टमोऽध्यायः ॥

Viveka Dṛṣṭi
